signalAssessment(record){const {period,rows,students:rows.length,hours:sum('absences'),minutes:absenceMinutes,days:sum('absenceDays'),unexcused:sum('unexcused'),open:sum('openAbsences'),late:sum('late'),possibleDays,scheduledMinutes,dayRate,denominator:scheduledMinutes,rate,rateBasis:scheduledMinutes?'Unterrichtsminuten':'betroffene
